- [ ] **Q2010 LOW The press harness presses a control when its live-row read throws, skipping the check that keeps a prod press on the record its gate approved (lh-silent-failure review of batch D, 2026-10-17; code read).** scripts/audit/press-every-control.mjs, in the block after `target = target.first()`: `target.evaluate(LIVE_ROW_TEXT…).catch(() => null)`, and a null result skips the record check and presses anyway. LIVE_ROW_TEXT never returns null itself (it returns ""), so null only means the read threw; that case should FAIL without pressing, like the "gone" branch. GUARD: a press-harness unit case where the row read throws and the press is refused. finding: lh-silent-failure@3a9981ec8#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2011 LOW When a poster cancels an open job carrying a direct offer, the offered Helpr is never told (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1891 fix, 2026-10-17, live bodies).** notify_on_job_update only notifies OLD.helper_id, so after the Q1891 trigger the offer just disappears from the Helpr's list; settle_one_off_jobs_for_banned_account already sends an "Offer closed" notice, poster_cancel_job does not. Also from the same review: if an admin or service role ever moves a cancelled job back to open, 'withdrawn' plus offered_to_helper_id is hidden by get_ranked_open_jobs / job_announceable_to / the saved-search paths (they treat only declined/expired as public) but shown by get_public_open_jobs, get_open_jobs_for_map and instant-job-match; mapping 'withdrawn' to 'expired' in jobs_reopen_retires_direct_offer closes it. And respond_to_direct_offer on a withdrawn offer now answers offer_not_pending ("isn't yours to respond to any more"); job_not_open would read better. Fix: an "Offer closed" notice to the offered Helpr in the cancel trigger path, the reopen mapping, the error ordering; PGlite cases. finding: lh-money-escrow@0f26cf3f1#5 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q1999 LOW The Work Record's Active Period can name a month that has not happened (sec-b, 2026-10-17).** workDayOf (src/lib/workRecordDocument.ts) takes date_needed even when the job was completed earlier: helper-e2e's record (generated Oct 17, 2026) reads "October 2026 - November 2026" because job 7e52c767 (is_seed) was completed 2026-10-17 with date_needed 2026-11-08 (execute_sql). A real early completion does the same on an employment record. Fix: use the earlier of date_needed and the completed day (completed_at, Chicago), with a unit case in the existing workRecordDocument tests. - [ ] **Q1996 LOW Host Automation says "Calendar connected" even when the first sync it just ran failed (sec-b drive of Q1119, 2026-10-17, prod at 375).** Connect Calendar inserts the row, then fires str-ical-sync and never reads the answer (src/pages/profile/StrSettings.tsx, the "best-effort" fetch in the add mutation); the success toast "Calendar connected" shows while the card under it already says "Sync failed — reconnect?" with the feed error (measured: str-ical-sync answered 200 `ok:false` "We couldn't load that calendar feed..." for an unreachable feed; screenshot ~/.lh-shots/sec-b-1119-eli/375-str-connected.png). Fix: read the first sync's result and, when it reports a defect for this connection, say so in the toast ("Calendar saved, but we couldn't read it yet: ...") instead of a plain success. - [ ] **Q2023 LOW A member can mark their own Terms as accepted without tapping I Agree, because `authenticated` holds column UPDATE on profiles.terms_version_accepted (lh-authz-rls review of b8c647b3b, 2026-10-17, finding b; read from information_schema.column_privileges and the "Users can update their own safe fields" policy).** Writing the exact LATEST_TERMS_VERSION, or the current UTC month's label before a bump published later that month, skips the re-consent dialog; legal_acceptances (the real evidence) then shows no acceptance. The far-future variant is closed by tr_keep_newest_terms_acceptance (Q2024). Fix once the iOS 7119 review freeze lifts (it narrows a privilege): record acceptance through a SECURITY DEFINER RPC that writes profiles and legal_acceptances together with the server's own current version, and REVOKE UPDATE (terms_version_accepted, terms_accepted_at) ON profiles FROM authenticated; move TermsReconsentDialog, CompleteProfile and acceptCurrentTerms to the RPC first (an old build's direct PATCH would then fail, so it needs the compat floor raised too). Optional with it: log to error_logs when the trigger holds a future version, so a mislabelled bump is not silent. GUARD when built: a test that no client code writes terms_version_accepted directly, plus a live column-privilege check. finding: lh-authz-rls@b8c647b3b#b finding: lh-authz-rls@b8c647b3b#2 (its finding b) **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

- [ ] **Q2071 MEDIUM A released recurring date's pending $300+ visit Checkout stays payable (lh-money-escrow review of Q1802, 2026-10-17).** No path that releases a date (series_release_dates, give_up_series_dates, helper_cancel_booking, end_recurring_series; live bodies read in pg_proc) touches recurring_visit_payments, and the charge-recurring-visits sweep closes a still-pending row only on its visit date or once the series is over. create-payment's recurring_visit action and stripe-webhook's settleRecurringVisitCheckout (handlers/settleRecurringVisitCheckout.ts:85-95) never check that the date is still held, so the poster can pay for a date nobody holds, and the visit-date sweep then refunds it less the card fee: a fee lost to our own release. Fix: the release path expires the pending row's Checkout and marks the row expired; the webhook refuses to settle a row whose date has no holder (full refund). - [ ] **Q2082 HIGH Jobs fee switch (owner 2026-10-18, confirmed twice): the POSTER pays 5% of the job price + $0.70 (card cost inside it), the Helpr keeps 100%, the Declutter rule; new jobs only, behind ONE server switch the owner flips after a native build + minSupportedBuild (lane jobs-fee, plan ~/.lh-wt/handoffs-1017/jobs-fee-plan.md).** Built so far: slice 1 the server-only stamp jobs.poster_fee_flat_cents + CHECK jobs_poster_fee_model_consistent (a 0c Helpr flat stamp only with the poster stamp, whose Helpr percent is 0) + extended enforce_jobs_fee_model_lock (20261017230824); slice 2 the shared rule _shared/posterJobFee.ts + client mirror, and helprPaysNoFee so every Helpr fee read prices a poster-fee job at 0 even when its select lacks helper_fee_percent. Slice 3a (ready/jobs-fee-1017-3): the switch row jobs_poster_fee created OFF (20261017234656); create-payment escrow on the poster-fee model when the switch is on (the "Service fee" line taxed like the job's labor per the tax memo, the poster stamp with the Helpr 0/0, PaymentIntent metadata, the model in the idempotency key; Pay in 4 = Klarna + Afterpay only at 9% + 70c inside the providers' limits); a stale-client refusal (the request must carry the fee model and amounts the app showed, src/lib/jobCheckout.ts buildFeeQuote, sent by Post a Job and the quote booking screens); every create-payment Checkout Session names its payment methods (card, Link, ACH) and turns Adaptive Pricing off; the Post a Job checkout shows the fee from the same switch; a non-US card that pays any checkout pages ops (owner: Radar rule blocks them); the apply and cancel dialogs show no Helpr fee line on a poster-fee job (review #4). Gift first (ready/jobs-fee-1017-5): a gift-funded checkout is on the poster-fee model; redeem_gift_card adds the stamped fee to the cost the gift pays first (20261018031451, PGlite 3x: $50 gift on a $40 job pays $42.70, leaves $7.30), the shortfall checkout keeps the fee stamp, Pay in 4 is refused with a gift, the app quotes the same shortfall (giftFirstPosterFee.test.ts). BEFORE THE FLIP (lh-money-escrow review of gift-first, #4; #1 closed by slice 4 below): a gift-funded job in a taxable category is charged no sales tax on labor or fee (the settle never touches Stripe, the shortfall line is non-taxable, and checkoutSessionCompleted's $0-tax alert skips gift shortfalls): tax the gift path, or refuse a gift on a taxable poster-fee job. Recurring (ready/jobs-fee-1017-6): a series is on the poster-fee model too; charge-recurring-visits charges every visit its own 5% + 70c at the series' percent, taxes the fee with the labor on a taxable category (a service_fee line in the same Stripe Tax calculation), stamps the visit with the poster stamp and Helpr 0/0, and divides the visit's rate by the taxed base; a $300+ visit is parked with its fee; Pay in 4 is refused on a series, and a Pay-in-4 stamp re-asserts recurrence_days IS NULL (recurring review #4). Refunds (ready/jobs-fee-1017-7, slice 4): a poster's own cancel of a poster-fee job (create-payment cancel_escrow and the void-cancelled-payments sweep) keeps Stripe's real card fee only, never the 5% + 70c (_shared/refundAmount.ts posterCancelKeptFeeCents + client mirror, the approved wording 'less the card-processing cost'); disputes and admin refunds already keep the card cost only on a poster-fee job (cardCostFeeCents is 0 there, so Stripe's real fee is kept). A crew's unfilled spots return their whole share of the fee on a poster-fee job and keep only that share of Stripe's fee, once (process-scheduled-payouts feeHoldsCardFee); the charge.refunded fallback notice names the card processing fee, not a service fee. Gift-first review #1 closes: the gift part of the fee carries no card cost and restore_gift_card_for_job returns it whole, the card part keeps only its own Stripe fee. BEFORE THE FLIP (lh-money-escrow review of slice 4, #2 #3 #5 #6 #8): (a) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1018-1; OWNER pop-up 2026-10-18 'J3': the tax on a kept amount is KEPT FROM THE REFUND, we never pay it): _shared/refundAmount.ts refundToPayerCents takes the charge's tax (chargeTaxCentsFor: the Checkout Session's total_details.amount_tax, since the edge's pinned API 2025-08-27.basil returns no PaymentIntent tax figure; a visit's stored tax_cents, with taxBaseCents = labor + tax so a legacy visit's untaxed card-cost fee carries none) and also keeps taxOnKeptCents = ceil(kept x tax / (captured - tax)) on everything it keeps (card fee, late-cancel fee, crew block fees, setup fee), so the tax Stripe's proportional reversal leaves collected is exactly what was kept; every pricing call passes it (cancel_escrow, admin_refund_dispute, admin_refund_general, execute-dispute-split, void-cancelled-payments, both charge-recurring-visits withholds), money-reconciliation's under-refund ceiling allows it, and the client mirror + dispute preview match; notices say it plainly ('$0.39 of that is the sales tax on what we kept, which isn't refunded'; the webhook fallback 'the card processing fee and the sales tax on what we kept'; visits 'the card processor's fee and any sales tax on it'). GUARD: src/test/refundKeepsTaxOnKeptAmounts.test.ts (a sweep proving never less than the tax left collected and at most 1c more, plus an inventory of every refundToPayerCents call that must pass taxCents; 5 @mutate RED), src/lib/refundAmount.parity.test.ts, the executed src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts taxed admin-refund case. LEFT: Q2148 (the crew unfilled-spot refund, the late-cancel hold capture, the toast, Shops/Declutter, the stale-visit poster-fee base); the owner's 'Q1981' (a poster who cancelled while the debit was pending keeps the strike when it fails) was already made impossible by design by Q2074 (no hire before the money arrives), and src/test/failedBankPaymentKeepsCancelStrike.test.ts guards that the failed-payment path never removes a strike; (b) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-8): money-reconciliation's under-refund ceiling takes posterCancelKeptFeeCents and selects poster_fee_flat_cents, so a poster-fee cancel that keeps the 5% + 70c pages (src/test/edge/money-reconciliation-stripe.test.ts, red on both @mutate lines); (c) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-9): with no balance transaction read, _shared/stripeFees.ts estimatedFeeCentsFor estimates a Klarna (5.99% + 30c) or Afterpay (6% + 30c) charge at its own rate (rates read from stripe.com/pricing/local-payment-methods 2026-10-18), by the charge's method or, unexpanded, when every allowed method is Pay-in-4 (src/lib/actualOrEstimatedFeeCents.test.ts); an ACH charge with no fee read still takes the card estimate, over-keeping from the poster (review LOW, barely reachable: a succeeded ACH charge has its fee); (d) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-10): the cancel toast prices the gift against src/lib/cancelRefundCopy.ts giftCostCents (budget + urgent + the stamped fee, redeem_gift_card's cost), passed by ActivityDialogs (CancellationDialog.giftToast.test.tsx, cancelRefundCopy.test.ts); (f) refundNotice's fallback (charge.refunded before the path's own notice, _shared/refundNotice.ts serviceCents 0 branch) calls a crew cancel's whole kept remainder 'the card processing fee' even when it includes block fees void-cancelled-payments withheld: subtract the crew's block fees before labelling the rest (slice-4 delta re-review #1, LOW). DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-11): when the words come from the job (no kept reason, no dispute or fee-withheld metadata), sendRefundNotice reads a cancelled leadless crew's crew_block_fees (the crews the sweep withholds them on) and the fallback names them on their own; a failed read sends no mislabelled notice and the webhook pages ops (src/test/edge/refundNotice.test.ts). Its review LOWs not fixed: a payee in the other Stripe mode leaves rows the sweep did not withhold unvoided (the notice over-counts; Part F would also pay them), and a member's deletion after settlement voids a row (a late notice under-counts); (e) DONE in slice 4: the cancel_escrow and sweep payment_refunds reason strings name the card processing fee on a poster-fee job (asserted in create-payment-poster-fee and void-cancelled-payments-fee-ledger tests). Gift-paid fee: a cancel restores it whole (PGlite src/test/pglite/posterFeeGiftFirst.pglite.mjs: $42.70 back on a $40 job + $2.70 fee, a half share $21.35). Still to build: the Pay-in-4 choice on the checkout screen (server side built); the job-address performance_location for tax sourcing (Stripe Checkout supports it per its docs, not yet measured on our API version); checkout-completed comparing amount_total and metadata to the stamp; engagement-automations revenue and the money-reconciliation poster-fee charge check; BEFORE THE FLIP items DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-4): a recurrence_days change on a poster-fee job is refused (20261018024950, PGlite 3x), the stored sales_tax_rate divides by budget + the taxed fee (_shared/salesTax.ts jobEscrowTaxableBaseCents), a TEST-mode Checkout Session with only klarna + afterpay_clearpay was created (API 2025-08-27.basil names the param payment_method_types; the 2026-09-30.preview version renamed it allowed_payment_method_types, so a Stripe API version bump must rename it), and poster_fee_flat_cents joined JOB_READABLE_COLUMN_LIST (measured live); the Helpr-fault refund (owner 10-18: a no-show or withdrawal refunds the poster in full and the Helpr owes the card cost; no job path detects Helpr fault today, so this needs a fault signal first; a ban-ended visit and a cancel after a Helpr withdrawal still keep Stripe's fee, slice-4 review #8); every display; Help Center + Terms (wording APPROVED by the owner 2026-10-18, shown only while the switch is on, Terms version bump). - [ ] **Q2083 HIGH Job sales-tax classification disagrees with the tax memo the owner adopted in place of a CPA (~/.lh-wt/handoffs-1017/tax-research-memo.md, Q4 table; relayed by the lead 2026-10-17; code read of supabase/functions/_shared/salesTax.ts TAXABLE_CATEGORIES on b5aae43d5).** Today 14 categories are taxed (assembly, handyman and the 12 trades, owner Q1831 "covered either way"). Per the memo only repairs/maintenance of MOVABLE property and cleaning of carpets/furniture/linens/clothing plus alterations are taxable (R.S. 47:301.3 items 5 and 7). OVER-collected (memo: not taxable): handyman on a house, electrical, plumbing, hvac, roofing, carpentry, flooring, concrete_masonry, fencing, pressure_washing (real property), assembly (memo: probably not, MEDIUM), welding (movable or not depends on the job). Correct: auto_repair, appliance_repair. UNDER-collected: carpet/rug/upholstery cleaning and laundry/alterations have no category (they post as cleaning or other, untaxed). Also sourcing: the job checkout and calculate-tax/charge-recurring-visits source tax by the BILLING address (address_source billing / customer_update address auto); the memo says performance_location = the job address (Q1727 is the narrower preview-vs-charge address gap; Q1843 the urgent bonus line, which follows the same rule). Fix: per-job 'repair of an item / cleaning of carpet, furniture or clothes' answer at post time (the memo's recommendation) driving the taxable codes, specific non-taxable Stripe codes for the rest, job-address sourcing; the owner reverses or keeps Q1831 first (over-collected tax must be remitted or refunded). The jobs-fee switch (Q2082) taxes the 5% + 70c fee with the job's own code when the job is taxable. GUARD when built: src/lib/salesTax.parity.test.ts asserting the memo's taxable set exactly, red on today's 14-category list. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the only commit naming it filed it; salesTax.ts TAXABLE_CATEGORIES still not matched to the memo.** **LANE B FINDINGS 2026-10-25 (owner chose Option 1, not built; scope cut by the lead):** build = at posting ask whether the job is repair of a movable item, or cleaning of carpet / furniture / clothes (plus alterations); tax only those; stop taxing house repairs and trades (handyman on a house, electrical, plumbing, hvac, roofing, carpentry, flooring, concrete_masonry, fencing, pressure_washing; assembly per the memo); source by the job address (performance_location), not billing; guard src/lib/salesTax.parity.test.ts matching the memo set exactly (taxed set today: _shared taxable list in enforce_funded_category_tax_class, 14 categories). MEASURED read-only (jobs.sales_tax_amount > 0, prod): handyman 38 jobs $99.94, ALL is_seed (test accounts); auto_repair 1 job $14.06 (1251173a, real; auto repair = a movable item, so it stays taxable under Option 1); recurring_visit_payments.tax_cents > 0: none. So tax collected on now-untaxed categories from real customers = $0.00; only seed rows. Owner rule 2026-10-25: no accountant or lawyer, so follow and cite the published law (La. R.S. 47:301 et seq., LDR rules) on what to do with tax collected on non-taxable services; that citation was NOT verified in this lane, so it is still owed. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

Root cause: every robot (nightly journeys, press-every-control, loading-states, prod audits, the local agent harnesses) runs the real app against prod, and track() (src/lib/analytics.ts) sent their events as people and fanned them out to PostHog; only Sentry replay (Q275/Q379) and page_load timing skipped automated browsers. Anonymous past rows carry no origin or user agent, and a deleted test account's rows are anonymised (user_id null), so the old anonymous rows cannot be split into robot and person: they are left unmarked, not guessed. THE REAL FUNNEL, from the accounts themselves (auth.users joined to profiles, is_seed false, last 30 days): 33 sign-ups, all since 2026-10-09 (gmail 16, yahoo 10, icloud 3, aol 2, Apple relay 1, outlook 1), 26 of them email-confirmed; 18 people applied to a job (25 applications); 3 people posted 12 jobs, 11 of them paid. Guards: src/test/analyticsTagsRobotTraffic.test.ts (8 @mutate lines, all KILLED) and the PGlite proof src/test/pglite/analyticsTagAutomated.pglite.mjs (ALL PASS 21, applied 3x; NEW_MIGRATION=skip 8 FAIL). Left open: the CDP harness in ~/.lh-e2e-shared (outside the repo) should set lh_automation before load; scripts/perf/inp-lab.mjs deliberately reports webdriver false (its rows are tagged only by Origin when it runs on a preview). Found while measuring, not fixed here (filed separately if wanted): payment_made fires on any /payment-success visit, 1892 of the rows have no job_id (src/pages/post-job/PaymentSuccess.tsx no-reference branch); first_payment_collected fires when the paid-job count is 0 (`<= 1`). Deletion keeps the tag (it lives in properties; scrub_analytics_actor_from_properties strips only actor-valued keys); seed rows untagged: 0 of 1655; PGlite proof extended (4 new checks, red without the migration). Analytics under-count only: no payment, escrow or navigation behaviour depends on it. For a $150 fee on a $50 card + $250 gift job it reads "...the $148.25 gift you used on it is available again. $101.75 of it paid the cancellation fee for your Helpr.": the $50 card payment kept for the fee is never mentioned, the $1.75 card fee is counted inside the "cancellation fee" the poster compares with the $150 the cancel dialog showed, and giftKeptReason joins the late fee and crew block fees under one total. - [ ] **Q2116 HIGH Quotes: a paid quote job can still fail to hire its quoter, and the poster is never told; fix before the 'quotes' switch leaves off (lh-money-escrow review of 28a5ed3d7, must-fix 2; re-scoped by the review of 8ef7cd25b against train J).** Train J (20261018024631, on main) removed the case this was filed for: an unfunded offer no longer lapses (expire_pending_direct_offers skips it) and its 48 h window restarts when the money arrives (trg_restart_direct_offer_clock_on_funding), so a late checkout or a slow ACH payment no longer outlives the offer. Still open: (1) the quoter can DECLINE the unfunded offer by calling respond_to_direct_offer directly (the app no longer shows unfunded offers, the RPC's decline branch has no funding check: a gap in J for every direct offer); the poster is then told "open to everyone again" about an unpaid job, can still pay it (create-payment never checks the offer), and the paid job opens to all at the agreed price with nobody hired; (2) money that arrives after the start cutoff (a late-settling ACH; create-payment has no date check): the clock restart lands direct_offer_expires_at in the past, the hire trigger logs offer_not_pending/offer_expired (the reason reads offer_expired where the start passed: name it job_date_has_passed), nobody is told, and auto-expire-jobs cancels and refunds about a day later (whether that refund keeps the card fee is not checked); (3) a quoter blocked, banned or shadowbanned between booking and payment, or one who never finishes setup inside the restarted 48 h: the job is paid and later opens to everyone, the poster never told the quoter was not hired; (4) the quote request screen says "Booked and paid" whenever the job is paid, hired or not. Fix: respond_to_direct_offer refuses a decline on an unfunded offer (job_not_funded); create-payment refuses a quote job whose offer is not pending or whose start has passed; every not-hired path tells the poster (and the request screen reads helper_id, not just payment). GUARD when built: steps in src/test/pglite/quoteHireOnPay.pglite.mjs (unfunded decline refused; late funding past the cutoff tells the poster) plus an edge test for the create-payment refusal. finding: lh-money-escrow@28a5ed3d7#2 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the 'quotes' switch is still testers only and the fix steps are not built; must land before quotes open.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

- [ ] **Q2102 MEDIUM Shops tax location and voided holds: Stripe Tax on a Shops checkout is computed from the BUYER's billing address, and a hold that is cancelled before capture may still leave tax recorded (lh-money-escrow review of c69866902, finding 5, 2026-10-17; NOT verified either way).** M1 (shop-checkout, automatic_tax with billing_address_collection required) taxes every pickup order where the buyer's card bills, but a pickup sale is likely sourced to where the buyer takes it (the shop), and parish rates differ. Stripe Tax also records a transaction when Checkout completes; whether a PaymentIntent cancelled before capture (decline, no answer, withdraw) reverses it is not stated in the docs read. DECIDED 2026-10-18 (owner accepted the tax memo ~/.lh-wt/handoffs-1017/tax-research-memo.md, via the lead): (a) tax at the pickup / service / drop-off address via performance_location on each line, never billing; (b) Checkout + automatic_tax records tax at AUTHORIZATION, so the held (manual-capture) order moves to the PaymentIntents tax flow, which records at capture, proved in a Stripe TEST sandbox first; (c) the buyer fee is taxed as its own line with the item's code and location when the item is taxable (never txcd_90050001). (a) BUILT 2026-10-18 on ready/shops-m1b-addr-1018 (shop_pickup_addresses, migration 20261018210400; shop-checkout puts the pickup address on every line as performance_location; src/test/edge/shop-checkout.test.ts "taxes every line at the shop's pickup address"); (b) still open. Built as Shops M1b before any real money flows (shops-s3 lane); M1 stays behind the off switch until then. To close: (a) the CPA says how a Louisiana pickup sale is sourced (CPA list beside CPA2/CPA3); if at the shop, pass the shop's address (Checkout shipping/tax address or a Tax Calculation) instead of the billing address; (b) one Stripe TEST-mode hold, cancelled uncaptured, then read Stripe Tax's transactions/reports to see whether its tax stays recorded; if it does, the release step reverses it. UPDATE 2026-10-18 (shops-s3, M1b part 1): (c) BUILT: the buyer fee is split pro rata over the items' tax codes, one fee line per code, never non-taxable (splitBuyerFeeByTaxCode, src/test/shopTaxCodes.test.ts + src/test/edge/shop-checkout.test.ts). - [~] **Q2151 MEDIUM Owner decisions left by the no-QR/no-code Declutter handover (lh-money-escrow review of wip/declutter-no-code-1018, 2026-10-18).** (a) A seller's "Picked up" on a door pickup (allowed from the booked time) makes the sale final at once, so the buyer cannot then report a problem (sale_final); the owner chose "completes when the buyer taps I got it or the seller taps Picked up": keep, or record the tap and let the sweep finish it after N hours unless the buyer objects? (b) A porch order whose seller never posts the "Left on porch" photo is never auto-paid (owner rule) and has no other clock: if both sides go quiet the money stays held forever; add a backstop (e.g. 72 h after the booked time: seller no-show refund, or an admin alert)? (c) Helpr delivery lost the 1 km allowance with the code: a drop-off pin more than 200 m off leaves the Helpr unable to mark it delivered (only the buyer's "I got it" or an admin finishes it); accept the photo beyond 200 m as held for admin? (d) Dead data left on purpose: declutter_handovers.code and helpr_tasks.dropoff_code are still drawn (NOT NULL) and unread; declutter_my_handover has no client caller any more. finding: lh-money-escrow@3fa85a301#2 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: waited on owner (now decided): For the no-code Declutter handover, which of the four open points (a)-(d) in this item do you want changed, especially (a) whether a seller's 'Picked up' should make the sale final at once?** **OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-21 (via lead; jobs builder queue after board 141):** (a) the seller's "Picked up" on a door pickup is recorded and the sweep finalizes it after 24 h unless the buyer reports a problem (the buyer's "I got it" still finalizes at once); (b) a porch order with no "Left on porch" photo and no "I got it" sends an admin alert 72 h after the booked time; (c) keep blocking delivered-marking beyond 200 m; (d) remove the dead code columns and RPC: declutter_handovers.code, helpr_tasks.dropoff_code, declutter_my_handover. **OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-21 (MASTER-LIST):** "(a) seller 'Picked up' finalizes after 24h unless buyer objects (buyer 'I got it' still immediate); (b) porch no-photo 72h -> ALERT STAFF; (c) drop-off >200m KEEP BLOCKING; (d) dead code cleanup." **(a) BUILT by board 141d (ready/declutter-refunds-141d, 20261026155251, 2026-10-23):** every handover, the seller's "Picked up" included, now pays the seller only once the sale is final, 24 h later, and the buyer can ask for a refund or report a problem until then (owner 2026-10-22 #2: "I got it" no longer ends the 24 h either). (b), (c), (d) not touched. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Owner decisions (b), (c) and (d) from the no-QR handover review are still unanswered.

- [ ] **Q2172 MEDIUM restore_helpr_fault_fee edge cases in the payout deduction (jobs-fee lane, 2026-10-19, lh-money-escrow review of c4ec33afb #5).** (a) release-payout's 'conflict' exit (Stripe has a transfer, no ledger row) restores the deduction although money may have moved with the fee held back, so the debt can be taken twice; (b) restore never runs for a (job, Helpr) with an older reversed row, so a later failed attempt loses the deduction; (c) an adopted stuck pending claim after a bill landed in between reads a deduction as paid that was never withheld; (d) a retry whose cap shrank (onboarding fee owed on the second run) gets the first, larger answer and the TS guard refuses forever (stuck payout). Also the open race from the fork: a run stopping before its claim can restore while another run's claim is being inserted. Re-review of b1730c932 sighted two of these again: #3 (release-payout and process-scheduled-payouts race; one run restores before its claim while the other already re-read the deduction, so the Helpr is withheld X now and charged X again; fix: take the debt after claimPayout) and #5 (= (d): clamp a repeat answer to the current cap and give back the excess). Sighted again by the exact-cents review (#5): the cap now drops by x mod 100 too (exact cents, the one fix-run 2026-10-19), which widens (d) slightly; same fix. finding: lh-money-escrow@c4ec33afb#5 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

A timed-out transfer Stripe did create leaves the debt reading unpaid, so the next payout collects it again (the Helpr charged twice). Fix: restore only on a definite refusal, as the other two paths; an executed edge test with a timeout error. finding: lh-money-escrow@49b9302a2#6 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the only commit naming it renumbered it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

- [ ] **Q2263 LOW Spent and Home's "Held for you" under-subtract gift-card cover on a poster-fee job (lh-money-escrow review of the Home money tiles, 2026-10-20, #4; pre-existing in spentRows).** src/lib/posterSpend.ts posterSpentCents caps the gift at budget + urgent_fee (`giftCents = Math.min(workCents, ...)`), but since 20261018031451 redeem_gift_card applies a gift to budget + urgent + the stamped service fee on a poster-fee job (jobs.poster_fee_flat_cents not null). A gift larger than budget + urgent but smaller than the full cost is subtracted short, so Spent and Held overstate by the part of the gift that paid the fee. - [~] **Q2272 MEDIUM Board 141 refund requests: build refund_requests + open/answer RPCs + answer clock + server-computed amount, with a money check (owner 2026-10-21: BUILD).** Today there is no refund-request object (Builder 5 map, 2026-10-21): a Jobs problem is a dispute filed straight to Helpr (DisputeDialog, rpc_open_dispute; 72-hour auto-settle), and a Declutter problem (declutter_report_problem) is decided only by an admin, all-or-nothing (no partial split exists). Board 141's amount picker, "Agree · refund $20, you take home the rest", "Come back and finish" and "Answer within 2 days or it goes to Helpr to decide" have no server behind them, so the board is PARKED (truth rule). Building it is a money feature: a refund_requests table with RLS, open/answer RPCs (require_feature + REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon), the answer clock, a server-computed refund amount via _shared/refundAmount.ts, and an lh-money-escrow review. Done when: a member can ask the other side for part or all of the money back on a job or Declutter order; the other side can agree, offer to finish or disagree, and an unanswered request goes to Helpr after 2 days; the amount is computed server-side (_shared/refundAmount.ts); RLS on refund_requests and the RPCs REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon; a PGlite proof and an lh-money-escrow review are recorded. **JOBS SIDE BUILT in branch ready/builder2h-jobs-141 (2026-10-21, owner: build both sides, the money moves automatically):** migration 20261021102943 (refund_requests + RLS, rpc_open_refund_request / rpc_answer_refund_request behind the new 'refund_requests' switch, 2-day clock, amount from the job's own price; Agree records a decided dispute decided_via 'agreement' with both parties named and an audit row; refund-request-sweep settles it through execute-dispute-split's service door, which moves money only on refund_agreement_for_dispute's proof; I don't agree / no answer opens a normal dispute; Come back and finish sends the existing date/time request; no payout_pending while a request is live; account deletion refused, Q2273). GUARDS: src/test/edge/refundAgreementGuard.test.ts (no money move without the other side's recorded agreement), src/test/edge/refundRequestHold.test.ts, src/test/refundRequestTakeHome.test.ts, src/test/pglite/refundRequests.pglite.mjs. Review fixes applied (Helpr must have the switch; no back-out, cancel or Helpr swap under a live request; poster can withdraw; changed Helpr closes the request; truthful amount copy). Lead follow-ups (2026-10-21): Q2326 built; an agreement where either party is banned, suspended, under a ban review or on a payout hold is never settled automatically (refund_party_held; the dispute goes to an admin, ops paged once; PGlite HD-*, guard src/test/refundComeBackAndHolds.test.ts). Still open here: the Declutter side (seller "Offer part of it", second slice). Q2298 (amount rule) answered and built: an agreed part refunds exactly the amount asked. **DECLUTTER SIDE NOT BUILT, blocked on owner decisions (builder2h, 2026-10-21, read from the migrations and declutter-money):** (1) board 141's Declutter screens are AFTER pickup ("Picked-up items are usually final. This one goes to the seller"), but declutter_report_problem refuses once an order is handed_over (sale_final: "The seller was paid, so this sale is final", 20261017055502); (2) the seller is paid at once: declutter_complete_handover inserts the declutter_payouts row 'pending' with next_attempt_at now() (20261017055505) and declutter-money sends it on its next run (*/10), so money for a refund after pickup would have to come back from the seller's connected account (no Declutter transfer-reversal path exists) or be absorbed by Helpr, or payouts would need a hold window after handover; (3) no partial refund exists: declutter_refunds is one row per payment intent (UNIQUE, ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING), its fee policies are whole-order (keep_buyer_fee raises refund_amount_unknown unless the whole order is refunded), the payout is items_cents minus fee debt, and declutter_order_refunds_recorded_cents / the whole-charge check know only full and delivery-fee refunds; (4) the tax on a partial Checkout refund is unchecked (Q1998). **DECIDED by the owner (2026-10-21, via the lead):** the buyer can report within 24 h of pickup, the seller payout is held until the order is final (Q2151a), and a partial refund is EXACTLY the amount offered. PAUSED by the owner (2026-10-21, via the lead: Jobs only for now); not started, not even after the Declutter rework lands. **DECLUTTER SIDE BUILT (board 141d, declutter-builder 2026-10-23, branch ready/declutter-refunds-141d, migration 20261026155251, switch declutter_refund_requests at testers):** the seller's payout waits until the sale is final (24 h after every handover, also after a seller's no; declutter_sale_final in declutter_payouts_due); the buyer asks within 24 h (declutter_ask_refund); the seller refunds in full (everything back except the card fee, the buyer's fee included, a delivery fee that paid a Helpr kept; the item goes back), offers part (EXACTLY the amount, below the item price; the buyer takes it or asks Louisiana Helpr) or says no (the buyer may report a problem for 24 h); no answer in 24 h on either side goes to an admin as a problem (cron declutter-refund-requests); every refund after the handover goes through declutter_refund_after_handover, which refuses a payout already tried/marked/sent and rebooks the payout (declutter_payouts.refunded_cents, the seller's fee debt put back and re-taken); the admin decides post-handover problems (declutter_admin_decide_problem). Also fixed: a replayed paid event for a handed-over sale used to record a full order_closed refund (declutter_mark_order_paid). GUARDS: src/test/declutterRefundRequests.test.ts, src/components/declutter/declutterRefundRequest.test.tsx, src/test/pglite/declutterRefundRequests.pglite.mjs (94 checks; RED without the migration). Download My Data gains the declutter_refund_requests section (20261026155252). Reviews: lh-money-escrow APPROVE-WITH-NOTES (should-fixes 1-3 applied), lh-authz-rls APPROVE-WITH-NOTES (should-fixes 1-3 applied). Owner calls with the built defaults: issue #3558 (filed under Q2151), ANSWERED 2026-10-23 (option 2): a full refund the seller agrees is HELD until the seller taps "I got it back" (status 'returning', declutter_refund_item_returned); no confirmation within 72 hours sends it to an admin (built 2026-10-26 in this migration; PGlite declutterRefundRequests 110/110, guard declutterRefundRequests.test.ts check 9). (1) and (2) kept as built. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Jobs refund requests are built but the refund_requests switch is still testers; open it for everyone (owner: ship on for everyone) after a live check.

Needs an owner decision: a 'forfeited' status + an admin action (with an audit row), or keep blocking. (b) The crew chargeback read blocks every roster member while the job is 'chargeback', including one already re-paid or removed; narrow it to the member's own unpaid share (chargeback_clawbacks per helper). (c) Fail-closed trade-off: a 'chargeback' job blocks unless dispute_status is 'dispute_lost', but a card dispute LOST on a job that already carried an internal dispute status keeps that status, so its Helpr stays blocked until someone clears it; a card-dispute outcome column (written by chargeDisputeCreated/Closed, never overwritten by internal statuses) would end it. (d) chargeback_clawbacks 'repay_failed' has no automatic exit: the repay_failed alert should tell ops to mark the row 'repaid' with the transfer id after paying by hand, as the tip re-drive alert does. Done when: (a) is decided (keep blocking), (b) a test of a re-paid crew member not blocked, red on today's read, (c) a card-dispute outcome column with a test that a job lost on top of an internal status does not block, (d) the repay_failed alert names the mark-repaid step (alert copy test). finding: lh-money-escrow@dbfe9ca9c0#5 **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Parts (b) re-paid crew not blocked, (c) card-dispute outcome column and (d) repay_failed alert naming are still to build.

Live proof: POST /rest/v1/rpc/rentals_public_listing {"p_slug":"lane-c-probe-no-such-listing"} with the anon key answered `HTTP 500 {"code":"P0002","message":"listing_not_found"}`. A 500 is retried by shouldRetryQuery and reads as a server failure in alerts. For an ordinary account the three named functions answer 403 first (feature_disabled, 42501), so members do not see it today. Fix (not started; touches money RPCs, so it needs lh-money-escrow + lh-authz-rls): restate each to raise `PT404` (PostgREST returns 404) or P0001, update declutter-checkout / shop-checkout (both map `P0002` to 404 today) and the tests that pin P0002 (src/test/migrationRaiseCodesPreserved.test.ts and others); guard: no client-callable function raises a P0 code other than P0001. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

- [ ] **Q2443 HIGH MUST before the next App Store build, and before ready/card-hold-on-1027 lands: an app build without src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts cannot complete a hire on a held card (Q2399; finding: lh-money-escrow@b67262b5b5#1).** Read 2026-10-27 (card-hold-dark lane, code read only, nothing run live): in 20261027185146 accept_job_offer, respond_to_direct_offer and rpc_group_member_confirm RAISE payment_not_captured for a READY Helpr on a 'held' job, and only the new web code (src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts withCardHoldCapture) answers that by calling create-payment capture_hold. A build already in the App Store has neither: its Accept calls accept_job_offer directly, has no copy for payment_not_captured (origin/main src/lib/lifecycleErrors.ts RPC_ERROR_COPY has no such key) and shows "Couldn't accept the job — please try again." (origin/main src/components/job-card/activityActions/useOfferHandlers.ts accept path), and because the RAISE rolls the call back no job_accept_pending / crew_confirm_pending row is written, so card-hold-sweep (completePendingAccept / completePendingCrewConfirms, which already capture and complete a pending accept once the Helpr is ready) never finishes it. Unless the Helpr accepts again from an up-to-date build (the web app), the offer can run out its response_deadline unanswered. FIX (server fallback, not built: it is not contained to those three functions): for a ready Helpr on a held job, record the pending row and return a success-pending answer instead of raising, so the sweep charges and completes the hire within one 10-minute run; create-payment capture_hold must then accept that answer as its proof that every gate passed (today it requires the payment_not_captured refusal), cardHoldCapture.ts must call capture_hold on it so new builds still hire at once, the crew answer needs a shape an old build's confirmCrewSpot does not throw on (it calls stampOrThrow on helper_confirmed_at unless action is pending_setup), and the PGlite proof (H5, H10, H12), src/test/edge/card-hold.test.ts and e2e/journeys/05-card-hold.spec.ts move with it; it needs its own lh-money-escrow pass. Done when: an accept_job_offer call with no capture_hold on a held job, as a ready Helpr, ends hired after one card-hold-sweep run (PGlite + edge test), red on 20261027185146 as it stands. STATUS 2026-10-27 (lead ruling, at the flip): no App Store version has ever been live and no member has an installed build, so no public client can hit this; for the flip it means only that the next App Store build must include src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts, which every build cut from main now does. Stays open until that iOS release ships. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2446 MEDIUM A test account's far-out card-hold checkout (setup mode, job more than 6 days out) is dropped by the TEST-mode webhook, so the job never lists (Q2399).** Read 2026-10-27 (code only): create-payment escrow mints a mode "setup" Checkout for a far-out held job (supabase/functions/create-payment/index.ts, holdPlan.kind "schedule"), and stripe-webhook applies a TEST-mode checkout.session.* event only when isRoutedTestCheckout is true (supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/index.ts, the verifiedByTestSecret branch); isRoutedTestCheckout (supabase/functions/_shared/stripeMode.ts) answers a setup session true only for metadata.kind "declutter_card", so the card-hold setup session is acknowledged as test_checkout_not_routed and the job stays unpaid. Live (real) posters are not affected (the live branch does not run that check). Hold-at-post (a job within 6 days, payment mode, metadata job_id with no kind) is routed. Fix: route a setup session whose metadata carries card_hold "1" and a job_id (testEventScope still refuses one that names a real account), with an edge test beside the isRoutedTestCheckout cases in src/test/edge/declutter-payments.test.ts; needs a money review. Done when: that test is red on today's isRoutedTestCheckout and green after. finding: lh-money-escrow@5cfa25a7c7#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2444 MEDIUM A held card for a far-out job of $300 or more (or a taxed category) drops off the listing about every 6.5 days (Q2399 round-2 money review #2; finding: lh-money-escrow@b0efb428d3#2).** Such a job is held at posting (it cannot be held off-session later); the 7-day authorization reaches the renewal window, offSessionAllowed refuses an off-session renewal (Q210b / taxed), and failHold sets payment_status 'failed', which unlists the job and asks the poster to pay again. A job posted 3 weeks out cycles 2-3 times. Fix to decide: ask the poster to renew on a Checkout page BEFORE the hold lapses while keeping the job listed, or charge such jobs at posting (today's escrow). Owner question filed 2026-10-26: issue #3658 (owner-needed). Done when: a $300+ job 3 weeks out stays listed until its hire with at most one poster action. done-when: test src/test/edge/card-hold.test.ts **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2445 MEDIUM The admin refund and Quick Refund success legs of journeys 04-admin-safety and 04-money-outcomes no longer run (Q2399 round-2 silent-failure review #6; finding: lh-silent-failure@b0efb428d3#6).** Once card_hold_at_post is on for the poster (test accounts or everyone; it lands off, 2026-10-27) an open job never holds captured money, so both legs assert the refusal on a held card and then skip as justified (e2e/skipAllowlist.ts). Done when: both refund legs run green on a hired job and their skip entries are removed. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2447 LOW A card captured at the hire whose hire is then refused leaves the job funded with nobody hired, and an expiry refunds it minus the card fee (Q2399 round-4 money review #4; finding: lh-money-escrow@756b4e7602#4).** create-payment capture_hold (and card-hold-sweep's pending accept) capture the held card after the Helpr's own accept passed every gate and refused only payment_not_captured; if the second run of the hire is then refused (the response deadline passing in the same seconds, a ban landing, a block), the job sits in 'escrow' unhired (ops is paged: "captured but the hire did not complete"). If nobody is hired after that, void-cancelled-payments refunds it as an unfilled paid job, keeping Stripe's card fee, but the charge happened only because of our race, so under the owner's own-mistake rule (2026-10-14) it should go back in full. Fix: mark the hold row (failure_code 'captured_not_hired') at that moment and give void-cancelled-payments an own-mistake full-refund branch for a closed job whose captured hold carries it (classified in src/test/refundsKeepTheCardFee.test.ts). Done when: an edge test drives capture -> refused hire -> expiry and the refund is the full charge. done-when: test src/test/edge/card-hold.test.ts **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

Two round-4 findings (the reserved gift on a failed hold, the cancel dialog's refund copy on a held job) came through exactly that gap; both are fixed in the card hold landing, but the next new payment_status would slip the same way. Fix: a second inventory in that guard for negated never-paid lists, each classified for 'held' (counts as paid or not). - [ ] **Q2449 LOW An admin has no way to cancel a held job, and settle-forward leaves a held test leftover deferred (Q2399 round-4 money review "not verified" + silent review #9; finding: lh-silent-failure@756b4e7602#9).** admin_refund_general refuses a held job (409) and tells the admin to cancel the job, but no admin cancel action was found in src/components/admin; scripts/e2e/settleForward.mjs refuses any payment_status but 'escrow', so a held, hired e2e leftover sits in its deferred list until the expiry sweep cancels it (no money moves: a held card is released, never charged). - [~] **Q2483 HIGH Three new start reminders for hired Helprs, each ON by default and switchable in Job reminders (owner 2026-10-29, pop-up via the lead).** OWNER DECISION: (a) "Your job starts in 2 hours" with the address and a Directions button; (b) morning of, 8 AM Central on the job day, "You have a job today at <time>", skipped when the job starts before 10 AM; (c) "Time to head out" at start time minus estimated travel time if On my way was not tapped, or 30 minutes before start with no travel estimate, merged with Starting soon so they never get two at once. Each at most once per job, never for cancelled or reassigned jobs, test accounts never counted; channels as Starting soon (in-app, push, SMS if opted in). Build on Q2482: one job_reminder_types row and one remind_* column each. FIX (branch wip/b78-start-reminders, stacked on wip/b68-helpr-reminder-choices; B74 then B78): migration 20261030035326 adds remind_two_hours / remind_morning_of / remind_head_out (NOT NULL DEFAULT true), three job_reminder_types rows, public.helpr_start_reminder_sends (one row per job, Helpr and reminder, so each goes once; deleted with the account), sweep_helpr_start_reminders (service_role only, cron every 5 minutes) and restates sms_kind_for from live (the 2-hour and head-out texts go as urgent as Starting soon). Measured on prod 2026-10-30 (read-only): no travel estimate exists before On my way is tapped (0 of 6 job_tracking rows carry eta_minutes), so every head-out uses the 30-minute rule; Q2485 tracks a travel-estimate source. Never two at once (lead 2026-10-29): head-out is carried by Starting soon while that switch is on; the 8 AM one is carried by the 2-hour one when that is due in the same run, within 15 minutes, or already went out, while the 2-hour switch is on (a 10:00 job gets just "Your job starts in 2 hours" at 8 AM). The 2-hour one is not sent to a solo Helpr who still owes the day-of confirm, because sweep_confirm_reminders_and_repost reposts that job at the same moment (live body read 2026-10-30); the guard holds the two tests equal both ways.
